│  Final Answer:                                                              │
│  ```python                                                                  │
│  """                                                                        │
│  connect_database.py                                                        │
│  -------------------                                                        │
│                                                                             │
│  Utility module to create a connection to a PostgreSQL database.            │
│                                                                             │
│  The public entry point is the :func:`connect_database` function which      │
│  returns a                                                                  │
│  live :class:`psycopg2.extensions.connection` object.  All connection       │
│  parameters                                                                 │
│  are passed explicitly – this makes the function easy to test and avoids    │
│  hidden                                                                     │
│  state.                                                                     │
│                                                                             │
│  Typical usage                                                              │
│  ~~~~~~~~~~~~~                                                              │
│                                                                             │
│  >>> from connect_database import connect_database,                         │
│  DatabaseConnectionError                                                    │
│  >>> try:                                                                   │
│  ...     conn = connect_database(                                           │
│  ...         host="localhost",                                              │
│  ...         port=5432,                                                     │
│  ...         user="my_user",                                                │
│  ...         password="s3cr3t",                                             │
│  ...         dbname="my_db",                                                │
│  ...     )                                                                  │
│  ... except DatabaseConnectionError as exc:                                 │
│  ...     print(f"Unable to connect: {exc}")                                 │
│  ... else:                                                                  │
│  ...     # Use the connection (remember to close it when done)              │
│  ...     with conn.cursor() as cur:                                         │
│  ...         cur.execute("SELECT version();")                               │
│  ...         print(cur.fetchone())                                          │
│  ...     conn.close()                                                       │
│  """                                                                        │
│                                                                             │
│  from __future__ import annotations                                         │
│                                                                             │
│  import logging                                                             │
│  from dataclasses import dataclass                                          │
│  from typing import Any, Optional                                           │
│                                                                             │
│  import psycopg2                                                            │
│  from psycopg2 import OperationalError, sql                                 │
│  from psycopg2.extensions import connection as PgConnection                 │
│                                                                             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  # Logging configuration                                                    │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)                                       │
│  if not logger.handlers:                                                    │
│      # Configure a very simple console logger if the application hasn't     │
│  set one up.                                                                │
│      handler = logging.StreamHandler()                                      │
│      formatter = logging.Formatter(                                         │
│          fmt="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s – %(message)s",            │
│          datefmt="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",                                       │
│      )                                                                      │
│      handler.setFormatter(formatter)                                        │
│      logger.addHandler(handler)                                             │
│      logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)                                          │
│                                                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  # Public exception                                                         │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  class DatabaseConnectionError(RuntimeError):                               │
│      """                                                                    │
│      Raised when a connection to the database cannot be established.        │
│                                                                             │
│      The original exception from ``psycopg2`` is stored in the              │
│  ``__cause__``                                                              │
│      attribute, allowing callers to inspect low‑level details if required.  │
│      """                                                                    │
│                                                                             │
│      def __init__(self, message: str, *, host: str, port: int, dbname:      │
│  str) -> None:                                                              │
│          super().__init__(message)                                          │
│          self.host = host                                                   │
│          self.port = port                                                   │
│          self.dbname = dbname                                               │
│                                                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  # Dataclass for connection parameters (optional but handy for type         │
│  checking)                                                                  │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)                                        │
│  class DBConfig:                                                            │
│      """                                                                    │
│      Immutable container for database connection parameters.                │
│                                                                             │
│      Using a dataclass makes it easy to pass a single object around while   │
│  still                                                                      │
│      keeping the function signature explicit for static analysis tools.     │
│      """                                                                    │
│                                                                             │
│      host: str                                                              │
│      port: int                                                              │
│      user: str                                                              │
│      password: str                                                          │
│      dbname: str                                                            │
│      sslmode: Optional[str] = None                                          │
│      connect_timeout: Optional[int] = None                                  │
│                                                                             │
│      def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:                                   │
│          """Return a dictionary suitable for ``psycopg2.connect``."""       │
│          params: dict[str, Any] = {                                         │
│              "host": self.host,                                             │
│              "port": self.port,                                             │
│              "user": self.user,                                             │
│              "password": self.password,                                     │
│              "dbname": self.dbname,                                         │
│          }                                                                  │
│          if self.sslmode:                                                   │
│              params["sslmode"] = self.sslmode                               │
│          if self.connect_timeout is not None:                               │
│              params["connect_timeout"] = self.connect_timeout               │
│          return params                                                      │
│                                                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  # Core function                                                            │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  def connect_database(                                                      │
│      host: str,                                                             │
│      port: int,                                                             │
│      user: str,                                                             │
│      password: str,                                                         │
│      dbname: str,                                                           │
│      *,                                                                     │
│      sslmode: Optional[str] = None,                                         │
│      connect_timeout: Optional[int] = None,                                 │
│  ) -> PgConnection:                                                         │
│      """                                                                    │
│      Create and return a new PostgreSQL database connection.                │
│                                                                             │
│      Parameters                                                             │
│      ----------                                                             │
│      host :                                                                 │
│          Database server hostname or IP address.                            │
│      port :                                                                 │
│          TCP port on which the PostgreSQL server is listening.              │
│      user :                                                                 │
│          Username used for authentication.                                  │
│      password :                                                             │
│          Password used for authentication.                                  │
│      dbname :                                                               │
│          Name of the database to connect to.                                │
│      sslmode :                                                              │
│          Optional SSL mode (e.g. ``require``).  If omitted, the default     │
│          ``psycopg2`` behaviour is used.                                    │
│      connect_timeout :                                                      │
│          Optional timeout in seconds for establishing the connection.       │
│                                                                             │
│      Returns                                                                │
│      -------                                                                │
│      psycopg2.extensions.connection                                         │
│          A live connection object.  The caller is responsible for closing   │
│  it                                                                         │
│          (preferably using a context manager or ``finally`` block).         │
│                                                                             │
│      Raises                                                                 │
│      ------                                                                 │
│      DatabaseConnectionError                                                │
│          If the connection cannot be established for any reason (network,   │
│          authentication, configuration, etc.).  The original ``psycopg2``   │
│          exception is attached as the ``__cause__`` of the raised error.    │
│      """                                                                    │
│      logger.debug(                                                          │
│          "Attempting to connect to PostgreSQL: host=%s port=%s dbname=%s    │
│  user=%s",                                                                  │
│          host,                                                              │
│          port,                                                              │
│          dbname,                                                            │
│          user,                                                              │
│      )                                                                      │
│                                                                             │
│      # Build the connection dictionary – this mirrors what                  │
│  ``psycopg2.connect`` expects.                                              │
│      conn_params: dict[str, Any] = {                                        │
│          "host": host,                                                      │
│          "port": port,                                                      │
│          "user": user,                                                      │
│          "password": password,                                              │
│          "dbname": dbname,                                                  │
│      }                                                                      │
│      if sslmode:                                                            │
│          conn_params["sslmode"] = sslmode                                   │
│      if connect_timeout is not None:                                        │
│          conn_params["connect_timeout"] = connect_timeout                   │
│                                                                             │
│      try:                                                                   │
│          conn = psycopg2.connect(**conn_params)                             │
│          logger.info(                                                       │
│              "Successfully connected to database '%s' on %s:%s as user      │
│  '%s'.",                                                                    │
│              dbname,                                                        │
│              host,                                                          │
│              port,                                                          │
│              user,                                                          │
│          )                                                                  │
│          return conn                                                        │
│      except OperationalError as exc:                                        │
│          # Log the low‑level error for debugging, then raise a              │
│  domain‑specific exception.                                                 │
│          logger.error(                                                      │
│              "Failed to connect to database '%s' on %s:%s – %s",            │
│              dbname,                                                        │
│              host,                                                          │
│              port,                                                          │
│              exc,                                                           │
│          )                                                                  │
│          raise DatabaseConnectionError(                                     │
│              f"Unable to connect to database '{dbname}' on                  │
│  {host}:{port}.",                                                           │
│              host=host,                                                     │
│              port=port,                                                     │
│              dbname=dbname,                                                 │
│          ) from exc                                                         │
│                                                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  # Convenience wrapper using the dataclass (optional)                       │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  def connect_using_config(config: DBConfig) -> PgConnection:                │
│      """                                                                    │
│      Connect to the database using a :class:`DBConfig` instance.            │
│                                                                             │
│      This thin wrapper simply forwards the values stored in *config* to     │
│      :func:`connect_database`.  It exists to make the API ergonomic when    │
│  the                                                                        │
│      configuration is already packaged in a dataclass.                      │
│                                                                             │
│      Parameters                                                             │
│      ----------                                                             │
│      config :                                                               │
│          An immutable configuration object.                                 │
│                                                                             │
│      Returns                                                                │
│      -------                                                                │
│      psycopg2.extensions.connection                                         │
│          The live connection object.                                        │
│      """                                                                    │
│      return connect_database(                                               │
│          host=config.host,                                                  │
│          port=config.port,                                                  │
│          user=config.user,                                                  │
│          password=config.password,                                          │
│          dbname=config.dbname,                                              │
│          sslmode=config.sslmode,                                            │
│          connect_timeout=config.connect_timeout,                            │
│      )                                                                      │
│  ```                                                                        │
│                                                                             │

│          Optional SSL mode (e.g. ``require``).  If omitted, the default     │
│          ``psycopg2`` behaviour is used.                                    │
│      connect_timeout :                                                      │
│          Optional timeout in seconds for establishing the connection.       │
│                                                                             │
│      Returns                                                                │
│      -------                                                                │
│      psycopg2.extensions.connection                                         │
│          A live connection object.  The caller is responsible for closing   │
│  it                                                                         │
│          (preferably using a context manager or ``finally`` block).         │
│                                                                             │
│      Raises                                                                 │
│      ------                                                                 │
│      DatabaseConnectionError                                                │
│          If the connection cannot be established for any reason (network,   │
│          authentication, configuration, etc.).  The original ``psycopg2``   │
│          exception is attached as the ``__cause__`` of the raised error.    │
│      """                                                                    │
│      logger.debug(                                                          │
│          "Attempting to connect to PostgreSQL: host=%s port=%s dbname=%s    │
│  user=%s",                                                                  │
│          host,                                                              │
│          port,                                                              │
│          dbname,                                                            │
│          user,                                                              │
│      )                                                                      │
│                                                                             │
│      # Build the connection dictionary – this mirrors what                  │
│  ``psycopg2.connect`` expects.                                              │
│      conn_params: dict[str, Any] = {                                        │
│          "host": host,                                                      │
│          "port": port,                                                      │
│          "user": user,                                                      │
│          "password": password,                                              │
│          "dbname": dbname,                                                  │
│      }                                                                      │
│      if sslmode:                                                            │
│          conn_params["sslmode"] = sslmode                                   │
│      if connect_timeout is not None:                                        │
│          conn_params["connect_timeout"] = connect_timeout                   │
│                                                                             │
│      try:                                                                   │
│          conn = psycopg2.connect(**conn_params)                             │
│          logger.info(                                                       │
│              "Successfully connected to database '%s' on %s:%s as user      │
│  '%s'.",                                                                    │
│              dbname,                                                        │
│              host,                                                          │
│              port,                                                          │
│              user,                                                          │
│          )                                                                  │
│          return conn                                                        │
│      except OperationalError as exc:                                        │
│          # Log the low‑level error for debugging, then raise a              │
│  domain‑specific exception.                                                 │
│          logger.error(                                                      │
│              "Failed to connect to database '%s' on %s:%s – %s",            │
│              dbname,                                                        │
│              host,                                                          │
│              port,                                                          │
│              exc,                                                           │
│          )                                                                  │
│          raise DatabaseConnectionError(                                     │
│              f"Unable to connect to database '{dbname}' on                  │
│  {host}:{port}.",                                                           │
│              host=host,                                                     │
│              port=port,                                                     │
│              dbname=dbname,                                                 │
│          ) from exc                                                         │
│                                                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  # Convenience wrapper using the dataclass (optional)                       │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  def connect_using_config(config: DBConfig) -> PgConnection:                │
│      """                                                                    │
│      Connect to the database using a :class:`DBConfig` instance.            │
│                                                                             │
│      This thin wrapper simply forwards the values stored in *config* to     │
│      :func:`connect_database`.  It exists to make the API ergonomic when    │
│  the                                                                        │
│      configuration is already packaged in a dataclass.                      │
│                                                                             │
│      Parameters                                                             │
│      ----------                                                             │
│      config :                                                               │
│          An immutable configuration object.                                 │
│                                                                             │
│      Returns                                                                │
│      -------                                                                │
│      psycopg2.extensions.connection                                         │
│          The live connection object.                                        │
│      """                                                                    │
│      return connect_database(                                               │
│          host=config.host,                                                  │
│          port=config.port,                                                  │
│          user=config.user,                                                  │
│          password=config.password,                                          │
│          dbname=config.dbname,                                              │
│          sslmode=config.sslmode,                                            │
│          connect_timeout=config.connect_timeout,                            │
│      )                                                                      │
│  ```                                                                        │

│                                                                             │
│  ## 2️⃣  Code des tests unitaires                                            │
│                                                                             │
│  Le fichier de tests a été créé sous **`tests/test_connect_database.py`**   │
│  :                                                                          │
│                                                                             │
│  ```python                                                                  │
│  # tests/test_connect_database.py                                           │
│  import unittest                                                            │
│  from unittest.mock import patch, MagicMock                                 │
│  from dataclasses import asdict                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  # Import du module à tester                                                │
│  from connect_database import (                                             │
│      connect_database,                                                      │
│      connect_using_config,                                                  │
│      DatabaseConnectionError,                                               │
│      DBConfig,                                                              │
│  )                                                                          │
│                                                                             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  # Helper : configuration valide utilisée dans plusieurs tests              │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  VALID_CONFIG = DBConfig(                                                   │
│      host="localhost",                                                      │
│      port=5432,                                                             │
│      database="mydb",                                                       │
│      user="myuser",                                                         │
│      password="secret",                                                     │
│      sslmode="prefer",                                                      │
│  )                                                                          │
│                                                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  class TestConnectDatabase(unittest.TestCase):                              │
│      """Tests unitaires de connect_database et connect_using_config."""     │
│                                                                             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      # 1️⃣  Cas de succès – connexion établie                                │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_connect_success(self, mock_connect):                          │
│          """Vérifie que la fonction renvoie l'objet connexion lorsqu'il     │
│  n'y a pas d'erreur."""                                                     │
│          # Le mock renvoie un objet MagicMock qui représente la connexion   │
│          mock_conn = MagicMock(name="psycopg2_connection")                  │
│          mock_connect.return_value = mock_conn                              │
│                                                                             │
│          # Appel de la fonction avec une configuration valide               │
│          conn = connect_database(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))                    │
│                                                                             │
│          # Assertions                                                       │
│          mock_connect.assert_called_once_with(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))       │
│          self.assertIs(conn, mock_conn)                                     │
│                                                                             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      # 2️⃣  Cas d’échec – psycopg2 lève une exception                        │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_connect_failure_raises_custom_error(self, mock_connect):      │
│          """Vérifie que DatabaseConnectionError est levée quand             │
│  psycopg2.connect échoue."""                                                │
│          # Simuler une exception psycopg2.OperationalError                  │
│          mock_connect.side_effect = Exception("cannot connect")             │
│                                                                             │
│          with self.assertRaises(DatabaseConnectionError) as cm:             │
│              connect_database(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))                       │
│                                                                             │
│          # Le message doit contenir les informations de la configuration    │
│          self.assertIn("Failed to connect to database", str(cm.exception))  │
│          mock_connect.assert_called_once_with(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))       │
│                                                                             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      # 3️⃣  Cas d’arguments manquants / invalides – validation interne       │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      def test_connect_missing_parameter(self):                              │
│          """Vérifie que la fonction lève TypeError lorsqu'un paramètre      │
│  obligatoire manque."""                                                     │
│          # Suppression du paramètre 'host'                                  │
│          incomplete_cfg = asdict(VALID_CONFIG)                              │
│          incomplete_cfg.pop("host")                                         │
│                                                                             │
│          with self.assertRaises(TypeError):                                 │
│              # Python lèvera TypeError avant même d'entrer dans la          │
│  fonction                                                                   │
│              connect_database(**incomplete_cfg)                             │
│                                                                             │
│      def test_connect_invalid_port_type(self):                              │
│          """Vérifie que la fonction lève ValueError quand le port n'est     │
│  pas un int."""                                                             │
│          bad_cfg = asdict(VALID_CONFIG)                                     │
│          bad_cfg["port"] = "not-an-int"                                     │
│                                                                             │
│          with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:                          │
│              connect_database(**bad_cfg)                                    │
│                                                                             │
│          self.assertIn("Port must be an integer", str(cm.exception))        │
│                                                                             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      # 4️⃣  Tests de la fonction d’assistance connect_using_config           │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_connect_using_config_success(self, mock_connect):             │
│          """Vérifie que connect_using_config transmet correctement la       │
│  dataclass."""                                                              │
│          mock_conn = MagicMock(name="psycopg2_connection")                  │
│          mock_connect.return_value = mock_conn                              │
│                                                                             │
│          conn = connect_using_config(VALID_CONFIG)                          │
│                                                                             │
│          mock_connect.assert_called_once_with(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))       │
│          self.assertIs(conn, mock_conn)                                     │
│                                                                             │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_connect_using_config_failure(self, mock_connect):             │
│          """Vérifie que connect_using_config propage                        │
│  DatabaseConnectionError."""                                                │
│          mock_connect.side_effect = Exception("network error")              │
│                                                                             │
│          with self.assertRaises(DatabaseConnectionError):                   │
│              connect_using_config(VALID_CONFIG)                             │
│                                                                             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      # 5️⃣  Vérification du logging (facultatif mais recommandé)             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      @patch("connect_database.logging")                                     │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_logging_on_success(self, mock_connect, mock_logging):         │
│          """Le logger doit être appelé avec le bon niveau INFO en cas de    │
│  succès."""                                                                 │
│          mock_connect.return_value = MagicMock()                            │
│          connect_database(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))                           │
│                                                                             │
│          mock_logging.info.assert_called_once()                             │
│          args, _ = mock_logging.info.call_args                              │
│          self.assertIn("Successfully connected to database", args[0])       │
│                                                                             │
│      @patch("connect_database.logging")                                     │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_logging_on_failure(self, mock_connect, mock_logging):         │
│          """Le logger doit être appelé avec le niveau ERROR en cas          │
│  d’échec."""                                                                │
│          mock_connect.side_effect = Exception("timeout")                    │
│          with self.assertRaises(DatabaseConnectionError):                   │
│              connect_database(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))                       │
│                                                                             │
│          mock_logging.error.assert_called_once()                            │
│          args, _ = mock_logging.error.call_args                             │
│          self.assertIn("Failed to connect to database", args[0])            │
│                                                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  # Lancement du runner lorsqu’on exécute directement le fichier             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  if __name__ == "__main__":                                                 │
│      unittest.main(verbosity=2)                                             │
│  ```                                                                        │
│                                                                             │
│  ### Points clés du design des tests                                        │
│                                                                             │
│  | Cas testé | Objectif | Méthode |                                         │
│  |-----------|----------|---------|                                         │
│  | **Succès** | S’assurer que la fonction renvoie l’objet connexion réel.   │
│  | Mock de `psycopg2.connect` → `MagicMock`. |                              │
│  | **Échec de connexion** | Vérifier la transformation de l’exception       │
│  native en `DatabaseConnectionError`. | `side_effect` → exception           │
│  générique. |                                                               │
│  | **Paramètres manquants / type invalide** | Garantir la validation        │
│  pré‑appel (type checking). | Suppression de clé / port non‑int →           │
│  `TypeError` / `ValueError`. |                                              │
│  | **Fonction d’assistance** | Couvrir le passage de la dataclass à la      │
│  fonction principale. | Même logique que les deux premiers cas. |           │
│  | **Logging** | S’assurer que les messages appropriés sont émis. | Mock    │
│  du module `logging`. |                                                     │
│                                                                             │

│  # tests/test_connect_database.py                                           │
│  import unittest                                                            │
│  from unittest.mock import patch, MagicMock                                 │
│  from dataclasses import asdict                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  # Import du module à tester                                                │
│  from connect_database import (                                             │
│      connect_database,                                                      │
│      connect_using_config,                                                  │
│      DatabaseConnectionError,                                               │
│      DBConfig,                                                              │
│  )                                                                          │
│                                                                             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  # Helper : configuration valide utilisée dans plusieurs tests              │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  VALID_CONFIG = DBConfig(                                                   │
│      host="localhost",                                                      │
│      port=5432,                                                             │
│      database="mydb",                                                       │
│      user="myuser",                                                         │
│      password="secret",                                                     │
│      sslmode="prefer",                                                      │
│  )                                                                          │
│                                                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  class TestConnectDatabase(unittest.TestCase):                              │
│      """Tests unitaires de connect_database et connect_using_config."""     │
│                                                                             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      # 1️⃣  Cas de succès – connexion établie                                │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_connect_success(self, mock_connect):                          │
│          """Vérifie que la fonction renvoie l'objet connexion lorsqu'il     │
│  n'y a pas d'erreur."""                                                     │
│          # Le mock renvoie un objet MagicMock qui représente la connexion   │
│          mock_conn = MagicMock(name="psycopg2_connection")                  │
│          mock_connect.return_value = mock_conn                              │
│                                                                             │
│          # Appel de la fonction avec une configuration valide               │
│          conn = connect_database(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))                    │
│                                                                             │
│          # Assertions                                                       │
│          mock_connect.assert_called_once_with(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))       │
│          self.assertIs(conn, mock_conn)                                     │
│                                                                             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      # 2️⃣  Cas d’échec – psycopg2 lève une exception                        │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_connect_failure_raises_custom_error(self, mock_connect):      │
│          """Vérifie que DatabaseConnectionError est levée quand             │
│  psycopg2.connect échoue."""                                                │
│          # Simuler une exception psycopg2.OperationalError                  │
│          mock_connect.side_effect = Exception("cannot connect")             │
│                                                                             │
│          with self.assertRaises(DatabaseConnectionError) as cm:             │
│              connect_database(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))                       │
│                                                                             │
│          # Le message doit contenir les informations de la configuration    │
│          self.assertIn("Failed to connect to database", str(cm.exception))  │
│          mock_connect.assert_called_once_with(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))       │
│                                                                             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      # 3️⃣  Cas d’arguments manquants / invalides – validation interne       │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      def test_connect_missing_parameter(self):                              │
│          """Vérifie que la fonction lève TypeError lorsqu'un paramètre      │
│  obligatoire manque."""                                                     │
│          # Suppression du paramètre 'host'                                  │
│          incomplete_cfg = asdict(VALID_CONFIG)                              │
│          incomplete_cfg.pop("host")                                         │
│                                                                             │
│          with self.assertRaises(TypeError):                                 │
│              # Python lèvera TypeError avant même d'entrer dans la          │
│  fonction                                                                   │
│              connect_database(**incomplete_cfg)                             │
│                                                                             │
│      def test_connect_invalid_port_type(self):                              │
│          """Vérifie que la fonction lève ValueError quand le port n'est     │
│  pas un int."""                                                             │
│          bad_cfg = asdict(VALID_CONFIG)                                     │
│          bad_cfg["port"] = "not-an-int"                                     │
│                                                                             │
│          with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:                          │
│              connect_database(**bad_cfg)                                    │
│                                                                             │
│          self.assertIn("Port must be an integer", str(cm.exception))        │
│                                                                             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      # 4️⃣  Tests de la fonction d’assistance connect_using_config           │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_connect_using_config_success(self, mock_connect):             │
│          """Vérifie que connect_using_config transmet correctement la       │
│  dataclass."""                                                              │
│          mock_conn = MagicMock(name="psycopg2_connection")                  │
│          mock_connect.return_value = mock_conn                              │
│                                                                             │
│          conn = connect_using_config(VALID_CONFIG)                          │
│                                                                             │
│          mock_connect.assert_called_once_with(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))       │
│          self.assertIs(conn, mock_conn)                                     │
│                                                                             │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_connect_using_config_failure(self, mock_connect):             │
│          """Vérifie que connect_using_config propage                        │
│  DatabaseConnectionError."""                                                │
│          mock_connect.side_effect = Exception("network error")              │
│                                                                             │
│          with self.assertRaises(DatabaseConnectionError):                   │
│              connect_using_config(VALID_CONFIG)                             │
│                                                                             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      # 5️⃣  Vérification du logging (facultatif mais recommandé)             │
│      #                                                                      │
│  --------------------------------------------------------------------- #    │
│      @patch("connect_database.logging")                                     │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_logging_on_success(self, mock_connect, mock_logging):         │
│          """Le logger doit être appelé avec le bon niveau INFO en cas de    │
│  succès."""                                                                 │
│          mock_connect.return_value = MagicMock()                            │
│          connect_database(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))                           │
│                                                                             │
│          mock_logging.info.assert_called_once()                             │
│          args, _ = mock_logging.info.call_args                              │
│          self.assertIn("Successfully connected to database", args[0])       │
│                                                                             │
│      @patch("connect_database.logging")                                     │
│      @patch("connect_database.psycopg2.connect")                            │
│      def test_logging_on_failure(self, mock_connect, mock_logging):         │
│          """Le logger doit être appelé avec le niveau ERROR en cas          │
│  d’échec."""                                                                │
│          mock_connect.side_effect = Exception("timeout")                    │
│          with self.assertRaises(DatabaseConnectionError):                   │
│              connect_database(**asdict(VALID_CONFIG))                       │
│                                                                             │
│          mock_logging.error.assert_called_once()                            │
│          args, _ = mock_logging.error.call_args                             │
│          self.assertIn("Failed to connect to database", args[0])            │
│                                                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  # Lancement du runner lorsqu’on exécute directement le fichier             │
│  #                                                                          │
│  -------------------------------------------------------------------------  │
│  -- #                                                                       │
│  if __name__ == "__main__":                                                 │
│      unittest.main(verbosity=2)                                             │
│  ```                                                                        │
│                                                                             │
│  ### Points clés du design des tests                                        │
│                                                                             │
│  | Cas testé | Objectif | Méthode |                                         │
│  |-----------|----------|---------|                                         │
│  | **Succès** | S’assurer que la fonction renvoie l’objet connexion réel.   │
│  | Mock de `psycopg2.connect` → `MagicMock`. |                              │
│  | **Échec de connexion** | Vérifier la transformation de l’exception       │
│  native en `DatabaseConnectionError`. | `side_effect` → exception           │
│  générique. |                                                               │
│  | **Paramètres manquants / type invalide** | Garantir la validation        │
│  pré‑appel (type checking). | Suppression de clé / port non‑int →           │
│  `TypeError` / `ValueError`. |                                              │
│  | **Fonction d’assistance** | Couvrir le passage de la dataclass à la      │
│  fonction principale. | Même logique que les deux premiers cas. |           │
│  | **Logging** | S’assurer que les messages appropriés sont émis. | Mock    │
│  du module `logging`. |                                                     │

│                                                                             │

│  specifications.                                                            │

└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── ✅ Agent Final Answer ───────────────────────────┐
│                                                                             │
│  Agent: Développeur                                                         │
│                                                                             │
│  Final Answer:                                                              │
│  ```python                                                                  │
│  """                                                                        │
│  connect_database.py                                                        │
│                                                                             │
│  Utility module to create a PostgreSQL database connection using psycopg2.  │
│                                                                             │
│  The public API consists of:                                                │
│      - DatabaseConnectionError: custom exception raised when a connection   │
│  cannot be                                                                  │
│        established.                                                         │
│      - connect(...): function that returns a live psycopg2 connection       │
│  object.                                                                    │
│                                                                             │
│  All public objects are exported via __all__ for clarity.                   │
│  """                                                                        │
│                                                                             │
│  from __future__ import annotations                                         │
│                                                                             │
│  import psycopg2                                                            │
│  from psycopg2 import OperationalError, extensions                          │
│  from typing import Any                                                     │
│                                                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  class DatabaseConnectionError(Exception):                                  │
│      """                                                                    │
│      Raised when a connection to the PostgreSQL database cannot be          │
│  established.                                                               │
│                                                                             │
│      Attributes                                                             │
│      ----------                                                             │
│      host : str                                                             │
│          Hostname or IP address of the PostgreSQL server.                   │
│      port : int                                                             │
│          TCP port of the PostgreSQL server.                                 │
│      dbname : str                                                           │
│          Name of the database to connect to.                                │
│      user : str                                                             │
│          Username used for authentication.                                  │
│      original_exception : Exception                                         │
│          The original exception caught from psycopg2 (or a subclass         │
│  thereof).                                                                  │
│      """                                                                    │
│                                                                             │
│      def __init__(                                                          │
│          self,                                                              │
│          host: str,                                                         │
│          port: int,                                                         │
│          dbname: str,                                                       │
│          user: str,                                                         │
│          original_exception: Exception,                                     │
│      ) -> None:                                                             │
│          self.host = host                                                   │
│          self.port = port                                                   │
│          self.dbname = dbname                                               │
│          self.user = user                                                   │
│          self.original_exception = original_exception                       │
│          message = (                                                        │
│              f"Unable to connect to PostgreSQL database '{dbname}' "        │
│              f"on {host}:{port} as user '{user}'. "                         │
│              f"Original error: {original_exception}"                        │
│          )                                                                  │
│          super().__init__(message)                                          │
│                                                                             │
│                                                                             │
│  def connect(                                                               │
│      host: str,                                                             │
│      port: int,                                                             │
│      dbname: str,                                                           │
│      user: str,                                                             │
│      password: str,                                                         │
│      *,                                                                     │
│      sslmode: str | None = None,                                            │
│      connect_timeout: int | None = None,                                    │
│      **kwargs: Any,                                                         │
│  ) -> extensions.connection:                                                │
│      """                                                                    │
│      Create and return a new psycopg2 connection to a PostgreSQL database.  │
│                                                                             │
│      Parameters                                                             │
│      ----------                                                             │
│      host : str                                                             │
│          Hostname or IP address of the PostgreSQL server.                   │
│      port : int                                                             │
│          TCP port of the PostgreSQL server.                                 │
│      dbname : str                                                           │
│          Name of the database to connect to.                                │
│      user : str                                                             │

│  control, logs, or memory dumps. | **Encourage callers to obtain            │
│  credentials from a secure secret store** (e.g., OS keyring, HashiCorp      │
│  Vault, AWS Secrets Manager) or from environment variables that are loaded  │
│  at runtime. Document this in the module’s docstring. |                     │
│  | No explicit masking of the password when an exception is raised. |       │
│  Stack traces could inadvertently include the password if the original      │
│  `psycopg2.Error` is logged. | When re‑raising `DatabaseConnectionError`,   │
│  **never include the original exception message** or any part of the        │
│  connection parameters. Keep the error message generic (e.g., “Unable to    │
│  connect to the database”). |                                               │
│  | No logging of connection attempts. | Good – avoids accidental            │
│  credential leakage. | If logging is added later, ensure the password is    │
│  **never logged** (use `logging.LoggerAdapter` with filtered extra          │
│  fields). |                                                                 │
│                                                                             │
│  ### 2.2 Transport Security (SSL/TLS)                                       │
│                                                                             │
│  | Observation | Impact | Recommendation |                                  │
│  |-------------|--------|----------------|                                  │
│  | `sslmode` is an optional keyword argument passed directly to             │
│  `psycopg2`. | The caller can enforce SSL (`require`, `verify-full`, etc.)  │
│  but the default is `None`, which may allow unencrypted connections. |      │
│  **Set a secure default**, e.g., `sslmode: str = "require"` (or             │
│  `"verify-full"` if server certificates are available). Document the        │
│  security implications of each mode. |                                      │
│  | No validation of `sslmode` values. | Passing an unsupported value will   │
│  raise a `psycopg2.Error`, which is caught and wrapped, but the root cause  │
│  may be obscured. | **Validate** that `sslmode` is one of the allowed       │
│  values (`disable`, `allow`, `prefer`, `require`, `verify-ca`,              │
│  `verify-full`). Raise `ValueError` early if not. |                         │
│                                                                             │
│  ### 2.3 Connection Timeout                                                 │
│                                                                             │
│  | Observation | Impact | Recommendation |                                  │
│  |-------------|--------|----------------|                                  │
│  | `connect_timeout` is optional and passed through unchanged. | If         │
│  omitted, the driver may wait indefinitely, leading to denial‑of‑service    │
│  under network failure. | **Provide a sensible default** (e.g.,             │
│  `connect_timeout: int = 10`). Document the rationale. |                    │
│                                                                             │
│  ### 2.4 Exception Handling & Information Leakage                           │
│                                                                             │
│  | Observation | Impact | Recommendation |                                  │
│  |-------------|--------|----------------|                                  │
│  | The wrapper catches any `psycopg2.Error` and raises                      │
│  `DatabaseConnectionError` with a generic message. | This is good for       │
│  preventing internal DB error details from leaking. | Ensure the original   │
│  exception is **not attached** to the new exception (avoid `raise           │
│  DatabaseConnectionError(...) from e`). If debugging is required, expose a  │
│  **debug flag** that can be toggled in a controlled environment. |          │
│  | No custom subclassing for specific error categories (e.g.,               │
│  authentication failure vs. network error). | Callers cannot differentiate  │
│  failure reasons without parsing the generic message. | Consider **adding   │
│  more granular custom exceptions** (e.g., `DatabaseAuthError`,              │
│  `DatabaseTimeoutError`) while still keeping messages generic for           │
│  production. |                                                              │
│                                                                             │
│  ### 2.5 Dependency Security                                                │
│                                                                             │
│  | Observation | Impact | Recommendation |                                  │
│  |-------------|--------|----------------|                                  │
│  | Dependency on `psycopg2` (C‑extension). | Vulnerabilities in the         │
│  underlying library could affect the wrapper. | **Pin the version** in      │
│  `requirements.txt` (e.g., `psycopg2>=2.9.9,<2.10`). Regularly run          │
│  `pip-audit` or similar tools to detect CVEs. |                             │
│  | No explicit verification of the `psycopg2` binary distribution. |        │
│  Supply‑chain attacks are possible if an untrusted wheel is installed. |    │
│  Use **hash‑based verification** (`pip install --require-hashes`) for       │
│  production environments. |                                                 │
│                                                                             │
│  ### 2.6 Miscellaneous                                                      │
│                                                                             │
│  * **No use of `eval`/`exec` or dynamic import** – safe.                    │
│  * **No file I/O** – no risk of accidental credential writes.               │
│  * **No global mutable state** – thread‑safe for import.                    │
│                                                                             │
│  ---                                                                        │
│                                                                             │
│  ## 3. Licensing Review                                                     │

│  **Add an explicit license header** (e.g., MIT, Apache‑2.0, BSD‑3) to make  │
│  downstream usage clear. |                                                  │
│  | **`psycopg2`** | LGPL‑2.1+ (or GPL‑compatible) – the binary package      │
│  `psycopg2-binary` is under the same license. | LGPL permits linking from   │
│  proprietary code, but you must **provide a way to replace the library**    │
│  (e.g., source distribution or dynamic linking). If you distribute the      │
│  module as part of a closed‑source product, ensure you comply with LGPL     │
│  obligations (provide object files or allow relinking). |                   │
│  | **Python standard library** | PSF License – compatible with all          │
│  open‑source licenses. | No issues. |                                       │
│                                                                             │
│  **Recommendations**                                                        │
│                                                                             │
│  1. **Add a license header** at the top of `connect_database.py`. Example   │

│                                                                             │
│     ```python                                                               │

│     ```                                                                     │
│                                                                             │
│  2. **Include a `LICENSE` file** in the repository that matches the         │
│  header.                                                                    │
│                                                                             │
│  3. If you intend to **redistribute** the module (e.g., as part of a        │
│  wheel), **document the LGPL dependency** and provide instructions for      │
│  obtaining the source of `psycopg2`.                                        │
│                                                                             │
│  4. Verify that any **transitive dependencies** (e.g., `psycopg2-binary`    │
│  pulling in `libpq`) have compatible licenses.                              │
